7583 — cable neutral grip lat pulldown

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 7583 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

Pull a neutral-grip handle down to your upper chest on a cable stack. It builds the lats and the muscles between your shoulder blades.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit at the lat pulldown station, thighs secured, and grab the neutral-grip handle.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 7583.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Adjustable cable station. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 7583.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit at the lat pulldown station, thighs secured, and grab the neutral-grip handle. 7583.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lean back slightly and brace your core. 7583.json#/instructions/1
  3. Pull the handle down to your upper chest, driving your elbows toward your ribs. 7583.json#/instructions/2
  4. Squeeze your shoulder blades together at the bottom. 7583.json#/instructions/3
  5. Let the handle rise slowly until your arms are fully extended. 7583.json#/instructions/4

7584 — cable kneeling high low anti rotation chop

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 7584 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: kneeling.

A kneeling cable chop that moves from high to low while your torso resists twisting. It trains the deep core and obliques to stay stable.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Kneel side-on to a high cable and hold the handle with both hands. Brace your core and keep your hips square.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 7584.json#/instructions/0, 7584.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Kneel side-on to a high cable and hold the handle with both hands.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 7584.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Settle the documented knee or knees on their support, with comfortable lower-leg placement; do not invent machine pad spacing or a missing half-kneeling side.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 7584.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Adjustable cable station. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 7584.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Kneel side-on to a high cable and hold the handle with both hands. 7584.json#/instructions/0
  2. Brace your core and keep your hips square. 7584.json#/instructions/1
  3. Chop the handle diagonally down across your body, arms mostly straight. 7584.json#/instructions/2
  4. Do not let your torso rotate; the arms and shoulders do the travel. 7584.json#/instructions/3
  5. Return slowly under control, finish your reps, then switch sides. 7584.json#/instructions/4

7585 — twist turn lift

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 7585 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

A floor core move that combines a twist with a leg or torso lift. It works the abs and obliques through rotation.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie on your back with your knees bent and arms extended.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 7585.json#/instructions/0

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 7585.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie on your back with your knees bent and arms extended. 7585.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lift your shoulders and legs off the floor. 7585.json#/instructions/1
  3. Twist your torso and bring your knees toward one side. 7585.json#/instructions/2
  4. Turn back through the centre and lift again. 7585.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat to the other side, keeping the movement smooth and controlled. 7585.json#/instructions/4

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A band chop in a wide stance where you resist twisting. It strengthens the abs and obliques.
